int(assay['tests_per_lab_per_run_small'])\n        tests_bulk = int(assay['tests_per_lab_per_run_bulk'])\n\n        annual_revenue_small = payment_per_run_per_lab * active_labs * RUNS_PER_YEAR_SMALL\n        annual_revenue_bulk = payment_per_run_per_lab * active_labs * RUNS_PER_YEAR_BULK\n        annual_reagent_cost_small = reagent_price * active_labs * tests_small * RUNS_PER_YEAR_SMALL / 1000\n        annual_reagent_cost_bulk = reagent_price * active_labs * tests_bulk * RUNS_PER_YEAR_BULK / 1000\n        annual_carrier_cost_small = carrier_cost * active_labs * RUNS_PER_YEAR_SMALL\n        annual_carrier_cost_bulk = carrier_cost * active_labs * RUNS_PER_YEAR_BULK\n        annual_margin_small = annual_revenue_small - annual_reagent_cost_small - annual_carrier_cost_small\n        annual_margin_bulk = annual_revenue_bulk - annual_reagent_cost_bulk - annual_carrier_cost_bulk\n        annual_margin_difference = annual_margin_bulk - annual_margin_small\n\n        assay_outputs.append({\n            'assay_id': assay['assay_id'],\n            'assay_name': assay['assay_name'],\n            'active_labs': active_labs,\n            'payment_per_run_per_lab_usd': money(payment_per_run_per_lab),\n            'billing_effective_month': billing_row['effective_month'],\n            'carrier_type': carrier_type,\n            'carrier_cost_usd': money(carrier_cost),\n            'reagent_price_per_1000_tests_usd': money(reagent_price),\n            'runs_per_year_small_kit': RUNS_PER_YEAR_SMALL,\n            'runs_per_year_bulk_kit': RUNS_PER_YEAR_BULK,\n            'tests_per_lab_per_run_small_kit': tests_small,\n            'tests_per_lab_per_run_bulk_kit': tests_bulk,\n            'annual_revenue_small_kit_usd': money(annual_revenue_small),\n            'annual_revenue_bulk_kit_usd': money(annual_revenue_bulk),\n            'annual_reagent_cost_small_kit_usd': money(annual_reagent_cost_small),\n            'annual_reagent_cost_bulk_kit_usd': money(annual_reagent_cost_bulk),\n            'annual_carrier_cost_small_kit_usd': money(annual_carrier_cost_small),\n            'annual_carrier_cost_bulk_kit_usd': money(annual_carrier_cost_bulk),\n            'annual_margin_small_kit_usd': money(annual_margin_small),\n            'annual_margin_bulk_kit_usd': money(annual_margin_bulk),\n            'annual_margin_difference_bulk_minus_small_usd': money(annual_margin_difference),\n        })\n\n    assay_outputs.sort(key=lambda row: row['assay_id'])\n\n    total_small = sum(row['annual_margin_small_kit_usd'] for row in assay_outputs)\n    total_bulk = sum(row['annual_margin_bulk_kit_usd'] for row in assay_outputs)\n    total_difference = total_bulk - total_small\n    absolute_difference = abs(total_difference)\n    decision = 'adopt_bulk_kit' if absolute_difference < DECISION_THRESHOLD_USD else 'keep_small_kit'\n\n    totals = {\n        'total_annual_margin_small_kit_usd': money(total_small),\n        'total_annual_margin_bulk_kit_usd': money(total_bulk),\n        'total_annual_margin_difference_bulk_minus_small_usd': money(total_difference),\n        'absolute_total_margin_difference_usd': money(absolute_difference),\n    }\n\n    report = {\n        'metadata': template['metadata'],\n        'analysis': {\n            'assumptions': {\n                'runs_per_year_small_kit': RUNS_PER_YEAR_SMALL,\n                'runs_per_year_bulk_kit': RUNS_PER_YEAR_BULK,\n                'carrier_cost_interpretation': 'carrier_cost_usd is per shipment, with one shipment per active lab per run.',\n                'annual_margin_formula': 'annual_revenue - annual_reagent_cost - annual_carrier_cost',\n                'decision_threshold_usd': DECISION_THRESHOLD_USD,\n                'decision_rule': 'If absolute total margin difference is strictly less than 7000 USD, recommend adopt_bulk_kit; otherwise recommend keep_small_kit.',\n            },\n            'assays': assay_outputs,\n            'totals': totals,\n            'recommendation': {\n                'decision': decision,\n                'decision_threshold_usd': DECISION_THRESHOLD_USD,\n                'absolute_total_margin_difference_usd': money(absolute_difference),\n            },\n        },\n        'justification': (\n            f\"Because the absolute total annual margin difference is ${money(absolute_difference):.2f}, \"\n            f\"which is {'less than' if absolute_difference < DECISION_THRESHOLD_USD else 'not less than'} \"\n            f\"the ${DECISION_THRESHOLD_USD:.2f} threshold, the recommendation is {decision}.\"\n        ),\n    }\n\n    with (ROOT / 'reagent_policy_report.json').open('w') as handle:\n        json.dump(report, handle, indent=2)\n        handle.write('\\n')\n\n    summary_lines = [\n        '# Reagent Policy Summary',\n        '',\n        f\"- Total Small-Kit Annual Margin:
